\end{aligned}$$

f91725b48122c15d356ecbed6850747ba1dc6dff: $$\color{#1971c2}V = V_{1} = V_{2} = \color{#2f9e44}12\space V$$

21025df59ee90c71cf40b0832201c400ce2596b4: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Q_{1} &= C_{1}V_{1} = 200*12 = \color{#2f9e44}2400\space\mu C \\
Q_{2} &= C_{2}V_{2} = 300*12 = \color{#2f9e44}3600\space\mu C
\end{aligned}$$

9ddcd77bc4518da70768e2cfa62162987cbd3cf1: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Ep_{1} &= \frac{1}{2}V_{1}Q_{1} = \frac{1}{2}*12*(24*10^{-4}) = \color{#2f9e44}14.4*10^{-4}\space J \\
Ep_{2} &= \frac{1}{2}V_{2}Q_{2} = \frac{1}{2}*12*(36*10^{-4}) = \color{#2f9e44}21.6*10^{-4}\space J
\end{aligned}$$

75f4231a9c7bc9db981043d3e0985db1ab3b0daa: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
C_{23} &= C_{2}+C_{3} = 20+10 = 30\space\mu F \\ \\
\frac{1}{C} &= \frac{1}{C_{1}}+\frac{1}{C_{23}}+\frac{1}{C_{4}} \\
&= \frac{1}{5}+\frac{1}{30}+\frac{1}{15} \\
&= \frac{9}{30} \\
C &= \frac{10}{3} \approx \color{#2f9e44}3.33\space\mu F
\end{aligned}$$

5796587243781629527602f6048054293770fe8d: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Q &= CV \\
&= 3.33*80 \\
&\approx \color{#2f9e44}266.4\space\mu C
\end{aligned}$$

11637ad5b6a213a953e0849f0f4f00d0bb26cfd8: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Q_{1} = Q_{4} &= Q \approx \color{#2f9e44}266.4\space\mu C \\
Q_{2} &= C_{2}V_{2} = 20*8.96 \approx \color{#2f9e44}179.2\space\mu C \\
Q_{3} &= C_{3}V_{3} = 10*8.96 \approx \color{#2f9e44}89.6\space\mu C
\end{aligned}$$

c7966df0ee48192d4b9e95f1d49d8c13d90040b8: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
V_{1} &= \frac{Q_{1}}{C_{1}} = \frac{266.4}{5} \approx \color{#2f9e44}53.28\space V \\
V_{4} &= \frac{Q_{2}}{C_{2}} = \frac{266.4}{15} \approx \color{#2f9e44}17.76\space V \\
V_{23} &= V-V_{1}-V_{4} = 80-53.28-17.76 \approx \color{#2f9e44}8.96\space V
\end{aligned}$$

41f4d4b6b3ae77e1d040148773de1a7e1608cc52: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Ep_{1} &= \frac{1}{2}C_{1}V_{1}^2 = \frac{1}{2}*(5*10^{-6})*53.28^2 \approx \color{#2f9e44}7.09*10^{-3}\space J \\
Ep_{2} &= \frac{1}{2}C_{2}V_{2}^2 = \frac{1}{2}*(2*10^{-5})*8.96^2 \approx \color{#2f9e44}8.02*10^{-4}\space J \\
Ep_{3} &= \frac{1}{2}C_{3}V_{3}^2 = \frac{1}{2}*10^{-5}*8.96^2 \approx \color{#2f9e44}4.01*10^{-4}\space J \\
Ep_{4} &= \frac{1}{2}C_{4}V_{4}^2 = \frac{1}{2}*(15*10^{-6})*17.76^2 \approx \color{#2f9e44}2.36*10^{-3}\space J
\end{aligned}$$

968d8b42f950dc265862442593c0f0de1f774728: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
\frac{1}{C_{34}} &= \frac{1}{C_{3}}+\frac{1}{C_{4}} \\
&= \frac{1}{25}+\frac{1}{100} \\
&= \frac{1}{20} \\
C_{34} &= 20\space\mu F \\ \\
C_{234} &= C_{2}+C_{34} \\
&= 140+20 \\
&= 160\space\mu F \\ \\
\frac{1}{C} &= \frac{1}{C_{1}}+\frac{1}{C_{234}} \\
&= \frac{1}{40}+\frac{1}{160} \\
&= \frac{1}{32} \\
C &= 32\space\mu C
\end{aligned}$$

07c10668b94ed546e43d5c583e3b5583358e9690: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Q &= CV \\
&= 32*100 \\
&= 3200\space \mu C
\end{aligned}$$

b07bf086a8eaf0aa11dc6aa9dea66e8d6b90fa46: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
Q_{1} &= Q = 3200\space\mu C \\
Q_{2} &= C_{2}V_{2} = 140*20 = 2800\space\mu C \\
Q_{3} = Q_{4} &= C_{34}*V_{34} = 20*20 = 400\space\mu C
\end{aligned}$$

5891b82f961c4aae14f1fbedf68d2a6c425f199b: $$\color{#1971c2}\begin{aligned}
V_{1} &= \frac{Q_{1}}{C_{1}} = \frac{3200}{40} = 80\space V \\ \\
V_{234} &= 100-80 = 20\space V \\
V_{2} &= V_{234} = 20\space V \\ \\
V_{3} &= \frac{Q_{3}}{C_{3}} = \frac{400}{25} = 16\space V \\
V_{4} &= \frac{Q_{4}}{C_{4}} = \frac{400}{100} = 4\space V
\end{aligned}$$
